```
Serco - Army/Navy Training Group sat on call with Mike  - Henry transitioned over to Serco along with 900+ NGC employees as part of the Serco acquisition of NGC's training portfolio. $550 million transaction. Henry worked at NGC for 2+ years prior to the transition. He is a portfolio manager in charge of now Serco's Training pursuits. Serco currently has 4 training contracts within the Navy. Navy Surface training. Navy Submarine Simulator are the two programs Henry supports. His core function is for growth within the 4 contracts and seeking new pursuits within the Army/Navy portfolio. Glenn Steely & Art Soucy are the main POCs in Rhode Island for the MISS submarine program. Henry liked what we have to offer and our current performance with NGC, still figuring processes within Serco. Set monthly tag-up, says he'll be more informed in July so set a meeting a month out.
```

**Status**: New Lead

**Note Text:**

```
NGC: DAF CW/INSITE 

Tag up with Chris. he has a need for a Linux System administrator in beavercreek for their unclassified network. In the FTC DevNet, we recreate a microcosm of our customer environments for 200 developers to write software.
This means we need DevOps/Kubernetes expertise in a heavy Linux SADM environment. Experience with Kubernetes, GitLab, Docker/Podman are all useful.
If they are not strong in Linux, the DevOps will not be good enough though…

This starts as Unclassified, but they eventually need to obtain a TS/SCI as we would eventually find mission focused roles on site at NASIC/NSIC for them.
It does give a great place for strong Linux SADM to develop their Kubernetes/DevOps knowledge for future use on site at NASIC in a DevOps focused team
```

### Note #320

**Date**: 2025-01-22
**Author**: Colton Scurry
**Department**: Atlanta
**Type**: Contact
**Action**: Appointment
**About**: John Bagby
**Status**: New Lead
**Program References**: GUAM:, SAIC:

**Source**: `E21D300B-EADF-90DE-DB06105B0B05A326.XLS` (Row 169)

**Note Text:**

```
SAIC: IBCS 

tag up with John and Steve. they have a guy we are payrolling that they interviewwed through IG. not a user of IG anymore as of yesterday morning. SAIC is taking over all long-term work for IBCS for both domestic and OCONUS. 

GUAM: Kuait: Germany, Poland with domestic teams in HSV,WSMR etc. customer is not happy with Northrop Grumman. the problem is NGC has had the contract for 11 years, so anyone with experience with the system either works for NGC or the client which is tough due to relationship. NGC only one porttion that we atre tracking with speece. the FSR's from NGC did not have the software experience to train the army folks on ground, whre saic is replacing the 4 resources with 2 FSE's that have 14G or 14A experience that will better serve the defense patrons of the army when knowing how to acutally train and troubleshoot the system. with the relatioship, NGC still has some logistics aspects of IBCS and needs to work/get along with the new SAIC team but is causing a riff for a lack of better term. 43 hires each year. each team of FSE's will be embedded with a Warrant officer who is just a very experienced FSE in the Army on some sort of Defesne System. All Oconus, 6 month contract to hire. First 4 weeks are spent in HSV training. It does not matter how long they have been working with patriot c2bmc thadd etc, they will train in huntsville with new SAIC requirements. All folks they have hired from NGC have bad habbits so training is essentail. NGC lost vision of the mission and Army is pissed. 

converting at $120k with $40k in bonuses/fringe benefits (housing stipend, hazard pay, COLA pay etc.) no rotation. 3 in March through spetmber. Tag up today to review onboarding with candidate and time to meet on-site in HSV with program director and logistics team.
```
